- [ ] **Step 3: Write the failing wiring tests (state machine, async audio, SCO, EXTRAS)**

These are the tests for everything Task 9's heading promises: A2 (async audio), A3 (state machine
and backoff reconnect), A4 (Bluetooth wanted vs active), A7 (half duplex in the extras) and A8
(audio problems reach the chat log). Two of spec §6's four regression tests live here
("Bluetooth after reconnect" and "no main-thread join in disconnect"); the third
("foreground kept across reconnect") is Task 12's, the fourth is stream B's.

```kotlin
// libraries/humla/src/test/java/se/lublin/humla/testutil/FakeAudio.kt
package se.lublin.humla.testutil

import android.content.Context
import se.lublin.humla.audio.AudioOutput
import se.lublin.humla.exception.AudioException
import se.lublin.humla.protocol.AudioHandler
import se.lublin.humla.protocol.HumlaTCPMessageListener
import se.lublin.humla.protocol.HumlaUDPMessageListener
import se.lublin.humla.session.AudioConfig
import se.lublin.humla.session.AudioHandlerFactory
import se.lublin.humla.session.AudioSessionParams
import se.lublin.humla.session.ManagedAudio
import se.lublin.humla.util.HumlaLogger
import java.util.concurrent.CopyOnWriteArrayList
import java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger

/** A pipeline that opens no device; shared by the service tests. */
class FakeAudio : ManagedAudio {
    val shutdownCalls = AtomicInteger()
    @Volatile var shutdownThread: String? = null
    @Volatile var shutdownGate: CountDownLatch? = null
    @Volatile var warningListener: ((String) -> Unit)? = null
    val targetIds = CopyOnWriteArrayList<Byte>()

    override val tcpListener: HumlaTCPMessageListener = object : HumlaTCPMessageListener.Stub() {}
    override val udpListener: HumlaUDPMessageListener = object : HumlaUDPMessageListener.Stub() {}
    override val currentBandwidth: Int = 12_345
    override fun setVoiceTargetId(id: Byte) { targetIds += id }
    override fun setWarningListener(listener: ((String) -> Unit)?) { warningListener = listener }
    override fun shutdown() {
        shutdownThread = Thread.currentThread().name
        shutdownGate?.await()
        shutdownCalls.incrementAndGet()
    }
}

class FakeAudioFactory : AudioHandlerFactory {
    val created = CopyOnWriteArrayList<FakeAudio>()
    val configs = CopyOnWriteArrayList<AudioConfig>()
    val sessionParams = CopyOnWriteArrayList<AudioSessionParams>()
    @Volatile var failWith: AudioException? = null

    override fun create(
        context: Context,
        logger: HumlaLogger,
        config: AudioConfig,
        params: AudioSessionParams,
        encodeListener: AudioHandler.AudioEncodeListener,
        outputListener: AudioOutput.AudioOutputListener,
    ): ManagedAudio {
        configs += config
        sessionParams += params
        failWith?.let { throw it }
        return FakeAudio().also { created += it }
    }
}
```

```kotlin
// libraries/humla/src/test/java/se/lublin/humla/testutil/HumlaServiceHarness.kt
package se.lublin.humla.testutil

import android.os.Bundle
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.Looper
import org.robolectric.Robolectric
import org.robolectric.Shadows.shadowOf
import org.robolectric.android.controller.ServiceController
import org.robolectric.shadows.ShadowLooper
import se.lublin.humla.HumlaService
import se.lublin.humla.model.Server
import se.lublin.humla.net.FakeTcpTransport
import se.lublin.humla.net.FakeTransports
import se.lublin.humla.net.HumlaConnection
import se.lublin.humla.net.HumlaTCPMessageType
import se.lublin.humla.protobuf.Mumble
import se.lublin.humla.session.ReconnectPolicy
import se.lublin.humla.util.HumlaException
import se.lublin.humla.util.HumlaObserver
import java.util.concurrent.CopyOnWriteArrayList

/**
 * Builds a [HumlaService] whose three collaborators are fakes and drives it through a real
 * [HumlaConnection] over [FakeTransports], so the tests exercise the service's own wiring and
 * not a mock of it.
 */
class HumlaServiceHarness(
    private val autoReconnect: Boolean = false,
    reconnectPolicy: ReconnectPolicy = ReconnectPolicy(
        baseDelayMillis = 10L,
        maxDelayMillis = 10L,
        maxAttempts = 3,
        maxJitterFraction = 0.0,
    ),
) {
    val transports = FakeTransports()
    val audioFactory = FakeAudioFactory()
    val devices = FakeCommunicationDevices()
    val mainLooper: ShadowLooper = shadowOf(Looper.getMainLooper())
    val warnings = CopyOnWriteArrayList<String>()

    private val controller: ServiceController<HumlaService> = Robolectric.buildService(HumlaService::class.java)
    val service: HumlaService = controller.get()

    init {
        service.connectionFactory = { listener ->
            HumlaConnection(listener, transports, Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()))
        }
        service.audioFactory = audioFactory
        service.communicationDevices = devices
        service.reconnectPolicy = reconnectPolicy
        controller.create()
        service.registerObserver(object : HumlaObserver() {
            override fun onLogWarning(message: String) { warnings += message }
        })
        service.configureExtras(
            Bundle().apply {
                putParcelable(HumlaService.EXTRAS_SERVER, Server(-1, "test", "127.0.0.1", 64738, "me", ""))
                putBoolean(HumlaService.EXTRAS_AUTO_RECONNECT, autoReconnect)
            },
        )
        mainLooper.idle()
    }

    fun destroy() {
        controller.destroy()
        mainLooper.idle()
    }

    /**
     * Under Robolectric the test thread *is* the main thread and the main looper is paused, so a
     * poll on main-thread state would deadlock. Every wait in this harness therefore polls state
     * owned by the protocol thread and then drains the main looper explicitly.
     */
    fun drainMainWhenPosted() {
        awaitUntil(description = "a task on the main looper") { !mainLooper.isIdle }
        mainLooper.idle()
    }

    /** Opens the socket of connection number [index] (0-based) and reports it established. */
    fun openSocket(index: Int): FakeTcpTransport {
        awaitUntil(description = "tcp transport $index") {
            transports.tcps.size > index && transports.tcps[index].connectThread != null
        }
        val tcp = transports.tcps[index]
        tcp.simulateConnected()
        awaitUntil(description = "connection $index established") { service.getConnection()?.isConnected == true }
        mainLooper.idle()
        return tcp
    }

    /**
     * Feeds the root channel, the session user and ServerSync, i.e. exactly what a server sends
     * before the session is usable, and drains the main looper.
     */
    fun synchronize(tcp: FakeTcpTransport, session: Int = 1) {
        tcp.simulateMessage(
            HumlaTCPMessageType.ChannelState,
            Mumble.ChannelState.newBuilder().setChannelId(0).setName("Root").build().toByteArray(),
        )
        tcp.simulateMessage(
            HumlaTCPMessageType.UserState,
            Mumble.UserState.newBuilder().setSession(session).setName("me").setChannelId(0).build().toByteArray(),
        )
        tcp.simulateMessage(
            HumlaTCPMessageType.ServerSync,
            Mumble.ServerSync.newBuilder().setSession(session).setMaxBandwidth(72_000).build().toByteArray(),
        )
        awaitUntil(description = "server sync parsed") { service.getConnection()?.isSynchronized == true }
        mainLooper.idle()
        check(service.getConnectionState() == HumlaService.ConnectionState.CONNECTED) {
            "expected CONNECTED, was ${service.getConnectionState()}"
        }
    }

    fun connectAndSynchronize(index: Int = 0): FakeTcpTransport {
        if (index == 0) service.connect()
        val tcp = openSocket(index)
        synchronize(tcp)
        return tcp
    }

    /** Fails connection [index] the way a dropped socket does, including the late close report. */
    fun failConnection(index: Int, error: HumlaException) {
        val connection = service.getConnection()
        transports.tcps[index].simulateFailure(error)
        awaitUntil(description = "connection $index torn down") { connection?.isConnected != true }
        transports.tcps[index].simulateSocketClosed()
        mainLooper.idle()
    }
}
```

```kotlin
// libraries/humla/src/test/java/se/lublin/humla/HumlaServiceSessionTest.kt
package se.lublin.humla

import android.media.AudioDeviceInfo
import com.google.common.truth.Truth.assertThat
import org.junit.After
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.runner.RunWith
import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
import se.lublin.humla.session.SessionState
import se.lublin.humla.testutil.HumlaServiceHarness
import se.lublin.humla.util.HumlaException
import java.time.Duration

@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
class HumlaServiceSessionTest {
    private lateinit var harness: HumlaServiceHarness

    @After
    fun tearDown() {
        if (::harness.isInitialized) harness.destroy()
    }

    private fun start(autoReconnect: Boolean = false): HumlaServiceHarness =
        HumlaServiceHarness(autoReconnect = autoReconnect).also { harness = it }

    private fun connectionError() =
        HumlaException("socket reset", HumlaException.HumlaDisconnectReason.CONNECTION_ERROR)

    @Test
    fun connectWalksDisconnectedToConnectingToConnected() {
        val h = start()
        assertThat(h.service.getSessionState().value).isEqualTo(SessionState.Disconnected())

        h.service.connect()
        h.mainLooper.idle()
        assertThat(h.service.getSessionState().value).isEqualTo(SessionState.Connecting)
        assertThat(h.service.getConnectionState()).isEqualTo(HumlaService.ConnectionState.CONNECTING)

        h.synchronize(h.openSocket(0))

        assertThat(h.service.getSessionState().value).isEqualTo(SessionState.Connected)
        assertThat(h.service.getConnectionState()).isEqualTo(HumlaService.ConnectionState.CONNECTED)
        assertThat(h.service.isConnected()).isTrue()
    }

    @Test
    fun aConnectionErrorWithAutoReconnectEntersConnectionLostAndKeepsTheWakeLock() {
        val h = start(autoReconnect = true)
        h.connectAndSynchronize()

        h.failConnection(0, connectionError())

        val state = h.service.getSessionState().value as SessionState.ConnectionLost
        assertThat(state.attempt).isEqualTo(1)
        assertThat(state.reconnectInMillis).isEqualTo(10L)
        assertThat(h.service.isReconnecting()).isTrue()
        assertThat(h.service.getConnectionState()).isEqualTo(HumlaService.ConnectionState.CONNECTION_LOST)
        assertThat(h.service.isWakeLockHeldForTest()).isTrue() // spec A3: only Disconnected releases it
    }

    @Test
    fun bluetoothScoIsRestartedAfterAReconnect() {
        // Spec §6 regression test: "Bluetooth after reconnect".
        val h = start(autoReconnect = true)
        h.devices.available[7] = AudioDeviceInfo.TYPE_BLUETOOTH_SCO
        h.connectAndSynchronize()
        h.service.enableBluetoothSco()
        assertThat(h.devices.selectCalls).containsExactly(7)
        assertThat(h.service.isBluetoothScoActive()).isTrue()

        h.failConnection(0, connectionError())
        assertThat(h.service.getSessionState().value).isInstanceOf(SessionState.ConnectionLost::class.java)
        assertThat(h.service.usingBluetoothSco()).isTrue() // the wanted flag survives the loss

        h.mainLooper.idleFor(Duration.ofMillis(10)) // backoff timer
        h.synchronize(h.openSocket(1))

        assertThat(h.devices.selectCalls).containsExactly(7, 7).inOrder()
        assertThat(h.service.isBluetoothScoActive()).isTrue()
    }

    @Test
    fun reconnectAttemptsAreExhaustedAndEndInDisconnected() {
        val h = start(autoReconnect = true)
        h.connectAndSynchronize()

        // A successful ServerSync resets the attempt counter, so exhausting the policy means
        // failing again before the session synchronizes. maxAttempts = 3: losses 1..3 schedule a
        // retry, loss 4 gives up.
        h.failConnection(0, connectionError())
        for (index in 1..2) {
            assertThat(h.service.getSessionState().value).isInstanceOf(SessionState.ConnectionLost::class.java)
            h.mainLooper.idleFor(Duration.ofMillis(10)) // backoff timer fires
            h.openSocket(index)
            h.failConnection(index, connectionError())
        }
        h.mainLooper.idleFor(Duration.ofMillis(10))
        h.openSocket(3)
        h.failConnection(3, connectionError())

        assertThat(h.service.getSessionState().value).isInstanceOf(SessionState.Disconnected::class.java)
        assertThat((h.service.getSessionState().value as SessionState.Disconnected).error).isNotNull()
        assertThat(h.service.isReconnecting()).isFalse()
        assertThat(h.service.isWakeLockHeldForTest()).isFalse()
        assertThat(h.warnings).contains(h.service.getString(R.string.reconnect_gave_up))
    }

    @Test
    fun aUserDisconnectGoesStraightToDisconnectedAndReleasesTheRoute() {
        val h = start(autoReconnect = true)
        h.devices.available[7] = AudioDeviceInfo.TYPE_BLUETOOTH_SCO
        h.connectAndSynchronize()
        h.service.enableBluetoothSco()

        h.service.disconnect()
        h.mainLooper.idle()

        assertThat(h.service.getSessionState().value).isEqualTo(SessionState.Disconnected())
        assertThat(h.service.isReconnecting()).isFalse()
        assertThat(h.service.isWakeLockHeldForTest()).isFalse()
        assertThat(h.devices.clearCalls).isEqualTo(1)
        assertThat(h.service.usingBluetoothSco()).isTrue() // the user's wish is not a session resource
    }

    @Test
    fun cancelReconnectStopsTheTimerAndEndsTheSession() {
        val h = start(autoReconnect = true)
        h.connectAndSynchronize()
        h.failConnection(0, connectionError())

        h.service.cancelReconnect()
        h.mainLooper.idleFor(Duration.ofMillis(100))

        assertThat(h.service.getSessionState().value).isInstanceOf(SessionState.Disconnected::class.java)
        assertThat(h.transports.tcps).hasSize(1) // no reconnect was attempted
    }
}
```

```kotlin
// libraries/humla/src/test/java/se/lublin/humla/HumlaServiceAudioTest.kt
package se.lublin.humla

import android.media.AudioDeviceInfo
import android.media.MediaRecorder
import android.os.Bundle
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.Looper
import com.google.common.truth.Truth.assertThat
import org.junit.After
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.runner.RunWith
import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
import se.lublin.humla.exception.AudioInitializationException
import se.lublin.humla.session.AudioController
import se.lublin.humla.testutil.HumlaServiceHarness
import se.lublin.humla.testutil.awaitUntil
import java.io.IOException
import java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean

@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
class HumlaServiceAudioTest {
    private lateinit var harness: HumlaServiceHarness

    @After
    fun tearDown() {
        if (::harness.isInitialized) harness.destroy()
    }

    private fun start(): HumlaServiceHarness = HumlaServiceHarness().also { harness = it }

    @Test
    fun audioIsBuiltOnTheControlThreadWithTheSessionUser() {
        val h = start()
        h.connectAndSynchronize()

        awaitUntil(description = "audio created") { h.audioFactory.created.size == 1 }
        assertThat(h.audioFactory.sessionParams[0].self.getName()).isEqualTo("me")
        assertThat(h.audioFactory.sessionParams[0].maxBandwidth).isEqualTo(72_000)
        assertThat(h.service.getCurrentBandwidth()).isEqualTo(12_345)
    }

    @Test
    fun disconnectDoesNotBlockTheMainThreadWhileAudioTearsDown() {
        // Spec §6 regression test: "no main-thread join in disconnect".
        val h = start()
        h.connectAndSynchronize()
        awaitUntil(description = "audio created") { h.audioFactory.created.size == 1 }
        val audio = h.audioFactory.created[0]
        val gate = CountDownLatch(1)
        audio.shutdownGate = gate

        val started = System.nanoTime()
        h.service.disconnect()
        h.mainLooper.idle()
        val blockedMillis = (System.nanoTime() - started) / 1_000_000

        awaitUntil(description = "shutdown started") { audio.shutdownThread != null }
        assertThat(audio.shutdownThread).isEqualTo(AudioController.THREAD_NAME)
        assertThat(audio.shutdownCalls.get()).isEqualTo(0) // still inside shutdown()

        // The main looper keeps running while the audio thread is stuck.
        val probeRan = AtomicBoolean(false)
        Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).post { probeRan.set(true) }
        h.mainLooper.idle()
        assertThat(probeRan.get()).isTrue()
        assertThat(blockedMillis).isLessThan(200)

        gate.countDown()
        awaitUntil(description = "shutdown finished") { audio.shutdownCalls.get() == 1 }
    }

    @Test
    fun audioCreationFailureIsLoggedAsAWarning() {
        val h = start()
        h.audioFactory.failWith = AudioInitializationException("no microphone")

        h.connectAndSynchronize()
        h.drainMainWhenPosted() // the failure is reported from humla-audio-control

        assertThat(h.warnings).contains("no microphone") // spec A8
        assertThat(h.service.getConnectionState()).isEqualTo(HumlaService.ConnectionState.CONNECTED)
    }

    @Test
    fun anAudioWarningReachesTheChatLog() {
        val h = start()
        h.connectAndSynchronize()
        awaitUntil(description = "audio created") { h.audioFactory.created.size == 1 }

        h.audioFactory.created[0].warningListener!!.invoke("microphone silenced by the system")
        h.drainMainWhenPosted()

        assertThat(h.warnings).contains("microphone silenced by the system") // spec A8
    }

    @Test
    fun aConnectionWarningReachesTheChatLog() {
        val h = start()
        h.connectAndSynchronize()

        h.transports.udps[0].simulateError(IOException("network unreachable"))
        h.drainMainWhenPosted()

        assertThat(h.warnings).contains(h.service.getString(R.string.udp_warning_thread_failed))
    }

    @Test
    fun theNineNewExtrasEndUpInTheAudioConfig() {
        val h = start()
        val extras = Bundle().apply {
            putString(HumlaService.EXTRAS_NOISE_SUPPRESSION, "rnnoise")
            putString(HumlaService.EXTRAS_ECHO_CANCELLATION, "webrtc")
            putString(HumlaService.EXTRAS_VAD_MODE, "probability")
            putFloat(HumlaService.EXTRAS_VAD_START, 0.7f)
            putFloat(HumlaService.EXTRAS_VAD_STOP, 0.4f)
            putInt(HumlaService.EXTRAS_VAD_HOLD_MS, 300)
            putBoolean(HumlaService.EXTRAS_ANDROID_NS, true)
            putBoolean(HumlaService.EXTRAS_ANDROID_AGC, true)
            putBoolean(HumlaService.EXTRAS_BLUETOOTH_WANTED, true)
        }

        assertThat(h.service.configureExtras(extras)).isFalse() // none of them needs a reconnect

        val config = h.service.getAudioConfigForTest()
        assertThat(config.noiseSuppression).isEqualTo("rnnoise")
        assertThat(config.echoCancellationMode).isEqualTo("webrtc")
        assertThat(config.vadMode).isEqualTo("probability")
        assertThat(config.vadStart).isEqualTo(0.7f)
        assertThat(config.vadStop).isEqualTo(0.4f)
        assertThat(config.vadHoldMs).isEqualTo(300)
        assertThat(config.androidNoiseSuppressor).isTrue()
        assertThat(config.androidAgc).isTrue()
        assertThat(h.service.usingBluetoothSco()).isTrue() // EXTRAS_BLUETOOTH_WANTED drives the router
    }

    @Test
    fun halfDuplexOnlyAppliesToPushToTalk() {
        val h = start()

        h.service.configureExtras(
            Bundle().apply {
                putBoolean(HumlaService.EXTRAS_HALF_DUPLEX, true)
                putInt(HumlaService.EXTRAS_TRANSMIT_MODE, Constants.TRANSMIT_VOICE_ACTIVITY)
            },
        )
        assertThat(h.service.getAudioConfigForTest().halfDuplex).isFalse()

        // Spec A7: switching the transmit mode at runtime must re-evaluate half duplex without
        // the caller having to resend EXTRAS_HALF_DUPLEX.
        h.service.configureExtras(
            Bundle().apply { putInt(HumlaService.EXTRAS_TRANSMIT_MODE, Constants.TRANSMIT_PUSH_TO_TALK) },
        )
        assertThat(h.service.getAudioConfigForTest().halfDuplex).isTrue()
    }

    @Test
    fun changingAnAudioExtraWhileConnectedRebuildsThePipeline() {
        val h = start()
        h.connectAndSynchronize()
        awaitUntil(description = "audio created") { h.audioFactory.created.size == 1 }

        h.service.configureExtras(Bundle().apply { putInt(HumlaService.EXTRAS_AUDIO_SOURCE, MediaRecorder.AudioSource.VOICE_COMMUNICATION) })

        awaitUntil(description = "audio rebuilt") { h.audioFactory.created.size == 2 }
        assertThat(h.audioFactory.configs[1].audioSource).isEqualTo(MediaRecorder.AudioSource.VOICE_COMMUNICATION)
        assertThat(h.audioFactory.created[0].shutdownCalls.get()).isEqualTo(1)
    }

    @Test
    fun theScoRouteBecomingActiveRebuildsThePipelineOffTheMainThread() {
        val h = start()
        h.devices.available[7] = AudioDeviceInfo.TYPE_BLUETOOTH_SCO
        h.connectAndSynchronize()
        awaitUntil(description = "audio created") { h.audioFactory.created.size == 1 }

        h.service.enableBluetoothSco()

        awaitUntil(description = "audio rebuilt for sco") { h.audioFactory.created.size == 2 }
        assertThat(h.audioFactory.configs[1].bluetoothActive).isTrue()
    }
}
```

```kotlin
    private var mTransmitMode = Constants.TRANSMIT_VOICE_ACTIVITY

    private var mVoiceTargetId: Byte = 0
    private lateinit var mWhisperTargetList: WhisperTargetList

    private lateinit var mWakeLock: PowerManager.WakeLock
    private lateinit var mHandler: Handler
    private lateinit var mCallbacks: HumlaCallbacks

    // Written on the main thread, read from the protocol thread (ModelHandler calls logInfo,
    // the audio output listener calls getUser), so every one of them is volatile.
    @Volatile private var mConnection: HumlaConnection? = null
    @Volatile private var mConnectionState = ConnectionState.DISCONNECTED
    @Volatile private var mModelHandler: ModelHandler? = null

    private lateinit var mStateMachine: SessionStateMachine
    private lateinit var mAudioController: AudioController
    private lateinit var mScoRouter: ScoRouter

    /** Current audio settings; replaced wholesale by [configureExtras] (spec §4). */
    private var mAudioConfig = AudioConfig()
    private lateinit var mInputMode: IInputMode

    private lateinit var mActivityInputMode: ActivityInputMode
    private lateinit var mToggleInputMode: ToggleInputMode
    private lateinit var mContinuousInputMode: ContinuousInputMode

    /** Test seam: builds the connection used by [connect]. */
    var connectionFactory: (HumlaConnection.HumlaConnectionListener) -> HumlaConnection = { HumlaConnection(it) }

    /** Test seam: builds the audio pipeline; stream B swaps in its own factory implementation. */
    var audioFactory: AudioHandlerFactory = DefaultAudioHandlerFactory()

    /** Test seam: null means "wrap the platform AudioManager in [onCreate]". */

    /** Test seam: the backoff used by the session state machine (spec A3). */
```

3. Replace `mConnectivityReceiver`'s body and add the reconnect timer:

```kotlin
    /** Listen for connectivity changes while waiting to reconnect, and retry immediately. */
    private val mConnectivityReceiver = object : BroadcastReceiver() {
        override fun onReceive(context: Context, intent: Intent) {
            if (mStateMachine.current !is SessionState.ConnectionLost) {
                unregisterConnectivityReceiver()
                return
            }
            if (!isOnline()) return
            Log.v(TAG, "Connectivity restored, attempting reconnect.")
            unregisterConnectivityReceiver()
            if (mStateMachine.connectivityRestored()) mHandler.post(mReconnectRunnable)
        }
    }

    private val mReconnectRunnable = Runnable {
        if (mStateMachine.reconnectTimerFired()) startSession()
    }

    private val mAudioControllerListener = object : AudioController.Listener {
        override fun onAudioStarted() = Unit

        /** Spec A8: a pipeline that cannot start is a chat-log warning, not a silent failure. */
        override fun onAudioFailed(message: String) = logWarning(message)

        /** Spec A8: microphone silencing and decoder errors reach the chat log. */
        override fun onAudioWarning(message: String) = logWarning(message)
    }

    private val mScoRouterListener = object : ScoRouter.Listener {
        override fun onScoActiveChanged(active: Boolean) {
            mAudioConfig = mAudioConfig.copy(bluetoothActive = active)
            // Posts to humla-audio-control; never joins on the main thread (spec A2).
            mAudioController.reconfigure(mAudioConfig, mInputMode)
        }

        override fun onScoUnavailable() = logWarning(getString(R.string.sco_unavailable))
    }
```

4. Replace `onCreate` and `onDestroy`:

```kotlin
    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        val powerManager = getSystemService(POWER_SERVICE) as PowerManager
        mWakeLock = powerManager.newWakeLock(PowerManager.PARTIAL_WAKE_LOCK, "Humla:HumlaService")
        mHandler = Handler(mainLooper)
        mCallbacks = HumlaCallbacks(mHandler)
        mStateMachine = SessionStateMachine(reconnectPolicy)
        mToggleInputMode = ToggleInputMode()
        mActivityInputMode = ActivityInputMode(0f) // FIXME: reasonable default
        mContinuousInputMode = ContinuousInputMode()
        mInputMode = mActivityInputMode
        mWhisperTargetList = WhisperTargetList()
        mAudioController = AudioController(
            this, this, { audioFactory }, mAudioInputListener, mAudioOutputListener,
            mAudioControllerListener, mHandler,
        )
        val devices = communicationDevices
            ?: AndroidCommunicationDevices(getSystemService(AUDIO_SERVICE) as AudioManager, mHandler)
        mScoRouter = ScoRouter(devices, mScoRouterListener)
        mConnectionState = ConnectionState.DISCONNECTED

        // initialize minidns dns lookup mechanisms
        AndroidUsingLinkProperties.setup(this)
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        mHandler.removeCallbacks(mReconnectRunnable)
        mStateMachine.disconnectRequested()
        unregisterConnectivityReceiver()
        stopScoRoute()
        mScoRouter.release()
        mAudioController.quit()
        if (mWakeLock.isHeld) mWakeLock.release()
        super.onDestroy()
    }
```

5. Replace `connect()` and `disconnect()` and add the session helpers:

```kotlin
    /** User-initiated connect. A connect while connecting or connected is ignored. */
    open fun connect() {
        if (!mStateMachine.connectRequested()) return
        startSession()
    }

    private fun startSession() {
        mHandler.removeCallbacks(mReconnectRunnable)
        mConnectionState = ConnectionState.CONNECTING
        mVoiceTargetId = 0
        mWhisperTargetList.clear()

        // Read the server before anything is built, so a misconfigured start allocates nothing.
        val server = checkNotNull(mServer) { "connect() requires EXTRAS_SERVER" }

        val connection = connectionFactory(this)
        mConnection = connection
        connection.setForceTCP(mForceTcp)
        connection.setUseTor(mUseTor)
        connection.setKeys(mCertificate, mCertificatePassword)
        connection.setTrustStore(mTrustStore, mTrustStorePassword, mTrustStoreFormat)

        val modelHandler = ModelHandler(this, mCallbacks, this, mLocalMuteHistory, mLocalIgnoreHistory)
        mModelHandler = modelHandler
        connection.addTCPMessageHandlers(modelHandler)

        mCallbacks.onConnecting()
        connection.connect(server)
    }

    override fun disconnect() {
        mHandler.removeCallbacks(mReconnectRunnable)
        mStateMachine.disconnectRequested()
        mConnection?.disconnect()
    }

    private fun isOnline(): Boolean {
        val cm = getSystemService(CONNECTIVITY_SERVICE) as ConnectivityManager
        val network = cm.activeNetwork ?: return false
        val capabilities = cm.getNetworkCapabilities(network) ?: return false
        return capabilities.hasCapability(NetworkCapabilities.NET_CAPABILITY_INTERNET)
    }

    private fun registerConnectivityReceiver() {
        try {
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            registerReceiver(mConnectivityReceiver, IntentFilter(ConnectivityManager.CONNECTIVITY_ACTION))
        } catch (e: IllegalArgumentException) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Error registering connectivity receiver: ${e.message}")
        }
    }

    private fun unregisterConnectivityReceiver() {
        try {
            unregisterReceiver(mConnectivityReceiver)
        } catch (e: IllegalArgumentException) {
            // Not registered; nothing to do.
        }
    }

    /**
     * Stops routing voice to a Bluetooth headset without forgetting that the user asked for it:
     * spec A4 says only [enableBluetoothSco]/[disableBluetoothSco] change the wanted flag.
     */
    private fun stopScoRoute() {
        val wanted = mScoRouter.wanted
        mScoRouter.wanted = false
        mScoRouter.apply()
        mScoRouter.wanted = wanted
    }
```

   `isOnline()` replaces the deprecated `activeNetworkInfo` checks; add
   `import android.net.NetworkCapabilities`.

6. Replace `onConnectionSynchronized`, `onConnectionDisconnected` and delete `setReconnecting`
   and `createAudioHandler` (the controller owns that lifecycle now):

```kotlin
    override fun onConnectionSynchronized() {
        val connection = mConnection ?: return
        if (!connection.isConnected) return // early disconnect
        val modelHandler = mModelHandler
        if (modelHandler == null) {
            Log.e(TAG, "onConnectionSynchronized: model handler is null")
            return
        }

        mStateMachine.synchronized()
        mConnectionState = ConnectionState.CONNECTED
        Log.v(TAG, "Connected")
        if (!mWakeLock.isHeld) mWakeLock.acquire()

        // Spec A4: a session exists again, so restore the route the user asked for.
        mScoRouter.apply()

        startAudio(connection, modelHandler)
        mCallbacks.onConnected()
    }

    private fun startAudio(connection: HumlaConnection, modelHandler: ModelHandler) {
        val self = try {
            modelHandler.getUser(connection.getSession())
        } catch (e: NotSynchronizedException) {
            null
        }
        if (self == null) {
            Log.e(TAG, "No session user after ServerSync; audio not started")
            return
        }
        val params = AudioSessionParams(
            self = self,
            maxBandwidth = connection.getMaxBandwidth(),
            codec = connection.getCodec(),
            targetId = mVoiceTargetId,
            inputMode = mInputMode,
        )
        mAudioController.start(mAudioConfig, params, connection)
    }

    override fun onConnectionDisconnected(e: HumlaException?) {
        if (e != null) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Error: ${e.message} (reason: ${e.reason.name})")
        } else {
            Log.v(TAG, "Disconnected")
        }

        val autoReconnect = mAutoReconnect && e != null &&
            e.reason == HumlaException.HumlaDisconnectReason.CONNECTION_ERROR
        val next = mStateMachine.lost(autoReconnect, e)

        mConnection = null
        mModelHandler = null
        mVoiceTargetId = 0
        mWhisperTargetList.clear()
        // Asynchronous: the capture and playback threads are joined on humla-audio-control,
        // never on the main thread (spec A2).
        mAudioController.shutdown()

        when (next) {
            is SessionState.ConnectionLost -> {
                // Spec A3: the wake lock, the SCO wanted flag, the mute/deafen state and the
                // foreground notification (MumlaService) all survive this transition.
                mConnectionState = ConnectionState.CONNECTION_LOST
                scheduleReconnect(next.reconnectInMillis)
            }
            is SessionState.Disconnected -> {
                mConnectionState = if (e != null) ConnectionState.CONNECTION_LOST else ConnectionState.DISCONNECTED
                if (autoReconnect) logWarning(getString(R.string.reconnect_gave_up))
                releaseSessionResources()
            }
            else -> Unit
        }

        mCallbacks.onDisconnected(e)
    }

    private fun scheduleReconnect(delayMillis: Long) {
        if (isOnline()) {
            Log.v(TAG, "Reconnecting in $delayMillis ms")
            mHandler.postDelayed(mReconnectRunnable, delayMillis)
        } else {
            // No point in burning attempts while there is no network; wait for it to come back.
            Log.v(TAG, "Offline; waiting for connectivity before reconnecting.")
            registerConnectivityReceiver()
        }
    }

    private fun releaseSessionResources() {
        mHandler.removeCallbacks(mReconnectRunnable)
        unregisterConnectivityReceiver()
        if (mWakeLock.isHeld) mWakeLock.release()
        stopScoRoute()
    }
```

7. Replace the extras mapping. Every branch that used to call `mAudioBuilder` now rebuilds
   `mAudioConfig`; the nine spec §4 keys are added; `EXTRAS_HALF_DUPLEX` keeps its own field so a
   later transmit-mode change re-evaluates it (spec A7):

```kotlin
    /**
     * Loads all defined settings from the given bundle into the HumlaService.
     * @return true if a reconnect is required for changes to take effect.
     */
    fun configureExtras(extras: Bundle): Boolean {
        var reconnectNeeded = false
        var config = mAudioConfig
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_SERVER)) {
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            mServer = extras.getParcelable(EXTRAS_SERVER)
            reconnectNeeded = true
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_AUTO_RECONNECT)) {
            mAutoReconnect = extras.getBoolean(EXTRAS_AUTO_RECONNECT)
        }
        // EXTRAS_AUTO_RECONNECT_DELAY is accepted and ignored: ReconnectPolicy owns the backoff.
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_CERTIFICATE)) {
            mCertificate = extras.getByteArray(EXTRAS_CERTIFICATE)
            reconnectNeeded = true
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_CERTIFICATE_PASSWORD)) {
            mCertificatePassword = extras.getString(EXTRAS_CERTIFICATE_PASSWORD)
            reconnectNeeded = true
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_DETECTION_THRESHOLD)) {
            mActivityInputMode.setThreshold(extras.getFloat(EXTRAS_DETECTION_THRESHOLD))
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_AMPLITUDE_BOOST)) {
            config = config.copy(amplitudeBoost = extras.getFloat(EXTRAS_AMPLITUDE_BOOST))
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_TRANSMIT_MODE)) {
            mTransmitMode = extras.getInt(EXTRAS_TRANSMIT_MODE)
            mInputMode = when (mTransmitMode) {
                Constants.TRANSMIT_PUSH_TO_TALK -> mToggleInputMode
                Constants.TRANSMIT_CONTINUOUS -> mContinuousInputMode
                Constants.TRANSMIT_VOICE_ACTIVITY -> mActivityInputMode
                else -> throw IllegalArgumentException()
            }
            config = config.copy(transmitMode = mTransmitMode)
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_INPUT_RATE)) {
            config = config.copy(inputSampleRate = extras.getInt(EXTRAS_INPUT_RATE))
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_INPUT_QUALITY)) {
            config = config.copy(targetBitrate = extras.getInt(EXTRAS_INPUT_QUALITY))
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_USE_OPUS)) {
            mUseOpus = extras.getBoolean(EXTRAS_USE_OPUS)
            reconnectNeeded = true
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_USE_TOR)) {
            mUseTor = extras.getBoolean(EXTRAS_USE_TOR)
            mForceTcp = mForceTcp or mUseTor // Tor requires TCP connections to work.
            reconnectNeeded = true
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_FORCE_TCP)) {
            mForceTcp = mForceTcp or extras.getBoolean(EXTRAS_FORCE_TCP)
            reconnectNeeded = true
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_CLIENT_NAME)) {
            mClientName = extras.getString(EXTRAS_CLIENT_NAME)
            reconnectNeeded = true
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_ACCESS_TOKENS)) {
            val tokens = extras.getStringArrayList(EXTRAS_ACCESS_TOKENS)
            mAccessTokens = tokens
            val connection = mConnection
            if (tokens != null && connection != null && connection.isConnected) {
                connection.sendAccessTokens(tokens)
            }
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_AUDIO_SOURCE)) {
            config = config.copy(audioSource = extras.getInt(EXTRAS_AUDIO_SOURCE))
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_AUDIO_STREAM)) {
            config = config.copy(audioStream = extras.getInt(EXTRAS_AUDIO_STREAM))
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_FRAMES_PER_PACKET)) {
            config = config.copy(targetFramesPerPacket = extras.getInt(EXTRAS_FRAMES_PER_PACKET))
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_TRUST_STORE)) {
            mTrustStore = extras.getString(EXTRAS_TRUST_STORE)
            reconnectNeeded = true
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_TRUST_STORE_PASSWORD)) {
            mTrustStorePassword = extras.getString(EXTRAS_TRUST_STORE_PASSWORD)
            reconnectNeeded = true
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_TRUST_STORE_FORMAT)) {
            mTrustStoreFormat = extras.getString(EXTRAS_TRUST_STORE_FORMAT)
            reconnectNeeded = true
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_HALF_DUPLEX)) {
            // Stored as requested; AudioConfig.halfDuplex applies the push-to-talk rule, so a
            // later EXTRAS_TRANSMIT_MODE change alone re-evaluates it (spec A7).
            config = config.copy(halfDuplexRequested = extras.getBoolean(EXTRAS_HALF_DUPLEX))
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_LOCAL_MUTE_HISTORY)) {
            mLocalMuteHistory = extras.getIntegerArrayList(EXTRAS_LOCAL_MUTE_HISTORY)
            reconnectNeeded = true
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_LOCAL_IGNORE_HISTORY)) {
            mLocalIgnoreHistory = extras.getIntegerArrayList(EXTRAS_LOCAL_IGNORE_HISTORY)
            reconnectNeeded = true
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_ENABLE_PREPROCESSOR)) {
            config = config.copy(preprocessorEnabled = extras.getBoolean(EXTRAS_ENABLE_PREPROCESSOR))
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_ECHO_CANCELLATION_METHOD)) {
            config = config.copy(legacyEchoCancellationMethod = extras.getString(EXTRAS_ECHO_CANCELLATION_METHOD) ?: "none")
        }
        // Spec §4 keys, read by stream B in DefaultAudioHandlerFactory.
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_NOISE_SUPPRESSION)) {
            config = config.copy(noiseSuppression = extras.getString(EXTRAS_NOISE_SUPPRESSION) ?: "none")
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_ECHO_CANCELLATION)) {
            config = config.copy(echoCancellationMode = extras.getString(EXTRAS_ECHO_CANCELLATION) ?: "none")
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_VAD_MODE)) {
            config = config.copy(vadMode = extras.getString(EXTRAS_VAD_MODE) ?: "amplitude")
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_VAD_START)) {
            config = config.copy(vadStart = extras.getFloat(EXTRAS_VAD_START))
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_VAD_STOP)) {
            config = config.copy(vadStop = extras.getFloat(EXTRAS_VAD_STOP))
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_VAD_HOLD_MS)) {
            config = config.copy(vadHoldMs = extras.getInt(EXTRAS_VAD_HOLD_MS))
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_ANDROID_NS)) {
            config = config.copy(androidNoiseSuppressor = extras.getBoolean(EXTRAS_ANDROID_NS))
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_ANDROID_AGC)) {
            config = config.copy(androidAgc = extras.getBoolean(EXTRAS_ANDROID_AGC))
        }
        if (extras.containsKey(EXTRAS_BLUETOOTH_WANTED)) {
            // Spec A4/P2: the persistent preference drives the wanted flag.
            mScoRouter.wanted = extras.getBoolean(EXTRAS_BLUETOOTH_WANTED)
            mScoRouter.apply()
        }

        val changed = config != mAudioConfig
        mAudioConfig = config
        if (changed && mAudioController.isRunning) {
            // Posts to humla-audio-control (spec A2); nothing is joined here.
            mAudioController.reconfigure(mAudioConfig, mInputMode)
            Log.i(TAG, "Audio subsystem reload requested after settings change.")
        }
        return reconnectNeeded
    }
```

   `configureExtras` can no longer actually throw `AudioException` — the reload is a post to the
   audio-control thread — but **keep `@Throws(AudioException::class)` on it**. `MumlaService.java`
   is still Java at this point and wraps its two calls in `try { … } catch (AudioException e)`;
   removing the declaration would make `javac` reject those blocks as unreachable. Declaring a
   checked exception that is never thrown is legal, and Task 12 drops both the declaration and the
   `catch` blocks when it converts `MumlaService`. No file outside this task is touched here.

8. Replace the Bluetooth surface (the `BluetoothScoReceiver` callbacks and

```kotlin
    /** Spec A4: what the user asked for, independent of what the platform currently routes. */
    override fun usingBluetoothSco(): Boolean = mScoRouter.wanted

    /** Spec A4: what the platform actually routes right now. */

    override fun enableBluetoothSco() {
        mScoRouter.wanted = true
        mScoRouter.apply()

    override fun disableBluetoothSco() {

```

9. Replace the state accessors and the audio accessors:

```kotlin
    override fun getSessionState(): StateFlow<SessionState> = mStateMachine.state

    override fun getConnectionState(): ConnectionState = mConnectionState

    override fun getConnectionError(): HumlaException? = when (val state = mStateMachine.current) {
        is SessionState.Disconnected -> state.error
        is SessionState.ConnectionLost -> state.error
        else -> null
    }

    override fun isReconnecting(): Boolean =
        mStateMachine.current is SessionState.ConnectionLost || mStateMachine.current == SessionState.Reconnecting

    override fun cancelReconnect() {
        mHandler.removeCallbacks(mReconnectRunnable)
        if (mStateMachine.cancelReconnect()) {
            mConnectionState = ConnectionState.CONNECTION_LOST
            releaseSessionResources()
        }
    }

    override fun getCurrentBandwidth(): Int = mAudioController.currentBandwidth

    override fun setVoiceTargetId(targetId: Byte) {
        require((targetId.toInt() and 0x1F.inv()) == 0) { "Target ID must be at most 5 bits." }
        mVoiceTargetId = targetId
        mAudioController.setVoiceTargetId(targetId)
        mCallbacks.onVoiceTargetChanged(VoiceTargetMode.fromId(targetId))
    }

    /**
     * Test seam: the settings the next pipeline would be built with. Public, not `internal`,
     * because `MumlaServiceForegroundTest` lives in the `app` module and Kotlin's `internal`
     * does not cross module boundaries.
     */
    fun getAudioConfigForTest(): AudioConfig = mAudioConfig

    /** Test seam: spec A3 requires the wake lock to survive a ConnectionLost. */
    fun isWakeLockHeldForTest(): Boolean = mWakeLock.isHeld
```

### Task 10: ChatMessageLog — a bounded chat log in the app service (spec A ownership, D5 acceptance)

**Files:**
- Create: `app/src/main/java/se/lublin/mumla/service/ChatMessageLog.kt`
- Test: `app/src/test/java/se/lublin/mumla/service/ChatMessageLogTest.kt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `se.lublin.mumla.service.IChatMessage` (existing Java; spec §4 forbids changing its shape).
- Produces (used by Task 12):
  - `class ChatMessageLog(capacity: Int = MAX_ENTRIES) { fun add(message: IChatMessage); fun snapshot(): List<IChatMessage>; fun clear(); val size: Int; companion object { const val MAX_ENTRIES = 500 } }`

Today `MumlaService.java:307` does `mMessageLog = new ArrayList<>()` and grows it without limit for
the lifetime of the connection, which is the leak D5's acceptance ("500 entries, oldest dropped")
points at. The log is A's file even though the symptom is chat's, because `MumlaService` is A's.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

```kotlin
// app/src/test/java/se/lublin/mumla/service/ChatMessageLogTest.kt
package se.lublin.mumla.service

import com.google.common.truth.Truth.assertThat
import org.junit.Test

class ChatMessageLogTest {
    private fun info(body: String): IChatMessage =
        IChatMessage.InfoMessage(IChatMessage.InfoMessage.Type.INFO, body)

    @Test
    fun keepsEveryMessageUntilTheCapacityIsReached() {
        val log = ChatMessageLog()

        repeat(ChatMessageLog.MAX_ENTRIES) { log.add(info("m$it")) }

        assertThat(log.size).isEqualTo(ChatMessageLog.MAX_ENTRIES)
        assertThat(log.snapshot().first().body).isEqualTo("m0")
        assertThat(log.snapshot().last().body).isEqualTo("m499")
    }

    @Test
    fun theFiveHundredAndFirstEntryEvictsTheOldest() {
        val log = ChatMessageLog()
        repeat(ChatMessageLog.MAX_ENTRIES) { log.add(info("m$it")) }

        log.add(info("m500"))

        assertThat(log.size).isEqualTo(ChatMessageLog.MAX_ENTRIES)
        assertThat(log.snapshot().first().body).isEqualTo("m1")
        assertThat(log.snapshot().last().body).isEqualTo("m500")
    }

    @Test
    fun snapshotIsNotAffectedByLaterAdditions() {
        val log = ChatMessageLog()
        log.add(info("first"))

        val snapshot = log.snapshot()
        log.add(info("second"))

        assertThat(snapshot.map { it.body }).containsExactly("first")
        assertThat(log.snapshot().map { it.body }).containsExactly("first", "second").inOrder()
    }

    @Test
    fun clearEmptiesTheLog() {
        val log = ChatMessageLog()
        log.add(info("gone"))

        log.clear()

        assertThat(log.size).isEqualTo(0)
        assertThat(log.snapshot()).isEmpty()
    }

    @Test
    fun aSmallerCapacityIsHonoured() {
        val log = ChatMessageLog(capacity = 2)

        log.add(info("a"))
        log.add(info("b"))
        log.add(info("c"))

        assertThat(log.snapshot().map { it.body }).containsExactly("b", "c").inOrder()
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement ChatMessageLog**

```kotlin
// app/src/main/java/se/lublin/mumla/service/ChatMessageLog.kt
/*
 * Copyright (C) 2026 The Mumla Authors
 *
 * This program is free software: you can redistribute it and/or modify
 * it under the terms of the GNU General Public License as published by
 * the Free Software Foundation, either version 3 of the License, or
 * (at your option) any later version.
 *
 * This program is distributed in the hope that it will be useful,
 * but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without even the implied warranty of
 * MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE.  See the
 * GNU General Public License for more details.
 *
 * You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License
 * along with this program.  If not, see <http://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
 */
package se.lublin.mumla.service

/**
 * The service's in-memory chat history, bounded at [capacity] entries; adding entry
 * [capacity] + 1 drops the oldest one (spec: stream A owns the log, stream D's D5 depends on the
 * bound). Written from the main thread and read by the chat UI, so reads hand out a snapshot.
 */
class ChatMessageLog(private val capacity: Int = MAX_ENTRIES) {
    private val entries = ArrayDeque<IChatMessage>(minOf(capacity, INITIAL_CAPACITY))

    init {
        require(capacity > 0) { "capacity must be positive" }
    }

    @Synchronized
    fun add(message: IChatMessage) {
        entries.addLast(message)
        while (entries.size > capacity) entries.removeFirst()
    }

    /** An immutable copy; later additions do not change it. */
    @Synchronized
    fun snapshot(): List<IChatMessage> = ArrayList(entries)

    @Synchronized
    fun clear() {
        entries.clear()
    }

    val size: Int
        @Synchronized get() = entries.size

    companion object {
        /** Spec D5: 500 entries, oldest dropped. */
        const val MAX_ENTRIES = 500
        private const val INITIAL_CAPACITY = 64
    }
}
```

**Files:**

- Convert: `app/src/main/java/se/lublin/mumla/service/MumlaReconnectNotification.java` → `MumlaReconnectNotification.kt`

**Interfaces:**

- Produces (used by Task 12):

  - `class MumlaReconnectNotification(context: Context, listener: OnActionListener) { fun show(error: String, autoReconnect: Boolean); fun hide(); companion object { fun show(context, error, autoReconnect, listener): MumlaReconnectNotification } }` — unchanged behavior, Kotlin, API < 31 branches deleted.

```kotlin
// app/src/test/java/se/lublin/mumla/service/MumlaConnectionNotificationTest.kt
package se.lublin.mumla.service

import android.app.ForegroundServiceStartNotAllowedException
import android.app.Service
import android.content.Intent
import android.os.IBinder
import com.google.common.truth.Truth.assertThat
import org.junit.After
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.runner.RunWith
import org.robolectric.Robolectric
import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
import org.robolectric.Shadows.shadowOf
import org.robolectric.android.controller.ServiceController

@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
class MumlaConnectionNotificationTest {
    /** A service with no behavior of its own; only its foreground state is under test. */
    class HostService : Service() {
        override fun onBind(intent: Intent?): IBinder? = null
    }

    private val controller: ServiceController<HostService> = Robolectric.buildService(HostService::class.java).create()
    private val service = controller.get()
    private val listener = object : MumlaConnectionNotification.OnActionListener {
        override fun onMuteToggled() = Unit
        override fun onDeafenToggled() = Unit
        override fun onOverlayToggled() = Unit
    }

    @After
    fun tearDown() {
        controller.destroy()
    }

    @Test
    fun showPutsTheServiceInTheForeground() {
        val notification = MumlaConnectionNotification.create(service, "Connecting", listener)

        assertThat(notification.show()).isTrue()

        assertThat(shadowOf(service).lastForegroundNotification).isNotNull()
        assertThat(shadowOf(service).isForegroundStopped).isFalse()
    }

    @Test
    fun updatingTheTextKeepsTheServiceInTheForeground() {
        val notification = MumlaConnectionNotification.create(service, "Connecting", listener)
        notification.show()

        notification.customContentText = "Connection lost – reconnecting…"
        assertThat(notification.show()).isTrue()

        assertThat(shadowOf(service).isForegroundStopped).isFalse()
    }

    @Test
    fun aRefusedForegroundStartIsReportedInsteadOfCrashing() {
        val notification = MumlaConnectionNotification.create(service, "Connecting", listener)
        notification.foregroundStarter = { _, _ ->
            throw ForegroundServiceStartNotAllowedException("not allowed from the background")
        }

        assertThat(notification.show()).isFalse() // spec A6: the caller decides what to do, no crash
    }

    @Test
    fun aSecurityExceptionIsReportedInsteadOfCrashing() {
        val notification = MumlaConnectionNotification.create(service, "Connecting", listener)
        notification.foregroundStarter = { _, _ -> throw SecurityException("missing FOREGROUND_SERVICE_MICROPHONE") }

        assertThat(notification.show()).isFalse()
    }

    @Test
    fun hideLeavesTheForegroundState() {
        val notification = MumlaConnectionNotification.create(service, "Connecting", listener)
        notification.show()

        notification.hide()

        assertThat(shadowOf(service).isForegroundStopped).isTrue()
    }
}
```

> If `ForegroundServiceStartNotAllowedException`'s constructor is not public in the compile SDK,
> throw `android.app.Service.startForeground`'s other documented refusal instead — a
> `SecurityException` — in that one test and note in the commit body that the
> `ForegroundServiceStartNotAllowedException` branch is covered by the `SecurityException` case
> plus the catch clause. Do not widen the production catch to `IllegalStateException`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Convert MumlaConnectionNotification to Kotlin and make the foreground start safe**

Delete `MumlaConnectionNotification.java`, create `MumlaConnectionNotification.kt` (GPL header
kept). The `Build.VERSION.SDK_INT <= M` title branch and the `>= O` notification-channel branch are
deleted (`minSdk = 31`); the `>= UPSIDE_DOWN_CAKE` (34) branches stay, because 31…33 are still in
range.

```kotlin
// app/src/main/java/se/lublin/mumla/service/MumlaConnectionNotification.kt
package se.lublin.mumla.service

import android.app.ForegroundServiceStartNotAllowedException
import android.app.Notification
import android.app.NotificationChannel
import android.app.NotificationManager
import android.app.PendingIntent
import android.app.PendingIntent.FLAG_CANCEL_CURRENT
import android.app.PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
import android.app.Service
import android.content.BroadcastReceiver
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Context.RECEIVER_NOT_EXPORTED
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.IntentFilter
import android.content.pm.ServiceInfo.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_TYPE_MICROPHONE
import android.os.Build
import android.util.Log
import androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat
import se.lublin.mumla.R
import se.lublin.mumla.app.DrawerAdapter
import se.lublin.mumla.app.MumlaActivity

/**
 * The ongoing notification that keeps the service in the foreground.
 *
 * [show] is safe to call repeatedly: it rebuilds the notification and re-enters the foreground.
 * It never throws — if the platform refuses the foreground start it returns false and the caller
 * (MumlaService) logs a warning and falls back to the reconnect notification (spec A6).
 */
class MumlaConnectionNotification private constructor(
    private val service: Service,
    contentText: String,
    private val listener: OnActionListener,
) {
    var customContentText: String = contentText
    var actionsShown: Boolean = false

    /** Test seam for the platform call that spec A6 requires to be survivable. */
    var foregroundStarter: (Int, Notification) -> Unit = { id, notification ->
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.UPSIDE_DOWN_CAKE) {
            service.startForeground(id, notification, FOREGROUND_SERVICE_TYPE_MICROPHONE)
        } else {
            service.startForeground(id, notification)
        }
    }

    private val notificationReceiver = object : BroadcastReceiver() {
        override fun onReceive(context: Context, intent: Intent) {
            when (intent.action) {
                BROADCAST_MUTE -> listener.onMuteToggled()
                BROADCAST_DEAFEN -> listener.onDeafenToggled()
                BROADCAST_OVERLAY -> listener.onOverlayToggled()
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Shows the notification, registers the action receiver and enters the foreground.
     * @return false if the platform refused the foreground start.
     */
    fun show(): Boolean {
        val filter = IntentFilter().apply {
            addAction(BROADCAST_DEAFEN)
            addAction(BROADCAST_MUTE)
            addAction(BROADCAST_OVERLAY)
        }
        try {
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.UPSIDE_DOWN_CAKE) {
                service.registerReceiver(notificationReceiver, filter, RECEIVER_NOT_EXPORTED)
            } else {
                service.registerReceiver(notificationReceiver, filter)
            }
        } catch (e: IllegalArgumentException) {
            // Thrown if the receiver is already registered.
            Log.d(TAG, "Notification receiver already registered")
        }
        return startForeground(buildNotification())
    }

    /** Hides the notification, unregisters the action receiver and leaves the foreground. */
    fun hide() {
        try {
            service.unregisterReceiver(notificationReceiver)
        } catch (e: IllegalArgumentException) {
            // Thrown if the receiver is not registered.
            Log.d(TAG, "Notification receiver was not registered")
        }
        service.stopForeground(Service.STOP_FOREGROUND_REMOVE)
    }

    private fun startForeground(notification: Notification): Boolean = try {
        foregroundStarter(NOTIFICATION_ID, notification)
        true
    } catch (e: ForegroundServiceStartNotAllowedException) {
        Log.w(TAG, "Foreground service start not allowed", e)
        false
    } catch (e: SecurityException) {
        Log.w(TAG, "Not permitted to start a microphone foreground service", e)
        false
    }

    private fun buildNotification(): Notification {
        val channelName = service.getString(R.string.connected)
        val channel = NotificationChannel(CHANNEL_ID, channelName, NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_DEFAULT)
        service.getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java).createNotificationChannel(channel)

        val builder = NotificationCompat.Builder(service, CHANNEL_ID)
            .setContentText(customContentText)
            .setSmallIcon(R.drawable.ic_stat_notify)
            .setPriority(NotificationCompat.PRIORITY_DEFAULT)
            .setCategory(NotificationCompat.CATEGORY_CALL)
            .setShowWhen(false)
            .setOngoing(true)

        if (actionsShown) {
            builder.addAction(R.drawable.ic_action_microphone, service.getString(R.string.mute), broadcast(BROADCAST_MUTE, 1))
            builder.addAction(R.drawable.ic_action_audio, service.getString(R.string.deafen), broadcast(BROADCAST_DEAFEN, 1))
            builder.addAction(R.drawable.ic_action_channels, service.getString(R.string.overlay), broadcast(BROADCAST_OVERLAY, 2))
        }

        val channelListIntent = Intent(service, MumlaActivity::class.java)
            .putExtra(MumlaActivity.EXTRA_DRAWER_FRAGMENT, DrawerAdapter.ITEM_SERVER)
        // FLAG_CANCEL_CURRENT ensures that the extra always gets sent.
        builder.setContentIntent(
            PendingIntent.getActivity(service, 0, channelListIntent, FLAG_CANCEL_CURRENT or FLAG_IMMUTABLE),
        )
        return builder.build()
    }

    private fun broadcast(action: String, requestCode: Int): PendingIntent {
        val intent = Intent(action).setPackage(service.packageName)
        return PendingIntent.getBroadcast(service, requestCode, intent, FLAG_CANCEL_CURRENT or FLAG_IMMUTABLE)
    }

    interface OnActionListener {
        fun onMuteToggled()
        fun onDeafenToggled()
        fun onOverlayToggled()
    }

    companion object {
        private val TAG = MumlaConnectionNotification::class.java.name
        private const val NOTIFICATION_ID = 1
        private const val CHANNEL_ID = "connected_channel"
        private const val BROADCAST_MUTE = "b_mute"
        private const val BROADCAST_DEAFEN = "b_deafen"
        private const val BROADCAST_OVERLAY = "b_overlay"

        /**
         * Creates a foreground Mumla notification for the given service.
         * @param service The service to register a foreground notification for.
         * @param listener A listener for notification actions.
         */
        @JvmStatic
        fun create(service: Service, contentText: String, listener: OnActionListener): MumlaConnectionNotification =
            MumlaConnectionNotification(service, contentText, listener)
    }
}
```

`MumlaService.java` is still Java at this point and calls `mNotification.setCustomContentText(text)`
and `mNotification.setActionsShown(true)`. Kotlin generates `setCustomContentText`/`setActionsShown`
for the two `var`s, so those call sites keep compiling; `mNotification.show()` now returns a
`boolean` that Java is free to ignore.

Delete `MumlaReconnectNotification.java`, create `MumlaReconnectNotification.kt` with the same

### Task 12: MumlaService — foreground driven by the session state, bounded chat log, half-duplex extras (spec A3, A6, A7)

**Files:**
- Convert then modify: `app/src/main/java/se/lublin/mumla/service/MumlaService.java` → `MumlaService.kt`
- Test: `app/src/test/java/se/lublin/mumla/service/MumlaServiceCharacterizationTest.kt`, `app/src/test/java/se/lublin/mumla/service/MumlaServiceForegroundTest.kt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `HumlaService` (Task 9: `getSessionState()`, `connect()`, `disconnect()`, `configureExtras`, `connectionFactory`), `SessionState` (Task 1), `ChatMessageLog` (Task 10), `MumlaConnectionNotification`/`MumlaReconnectNotification` (Task 11).
- Produces: `MumlaService` keeps every public member it has today (`IMumlaService`, `MumlaBinder`, `getMessageLog()`, `clearMessageLog()`, `onTalkKeyDown()`, …) so `MumlaActivity`, the fragments and streams D/P compile unchanged. New: `internal fun renderSessionState(state: SessionState)` (the single place that decides what the foreground notification shows) and `internal fun messageLogForTest(): ChatMessageLog`.

**Conversion convention used by this task.** `MumlaService.java` is ~690 lines and most of it is a
one-to-one translation (getters, the `IMumlaService` implementations, the overlay/hot-corner/TTS
plumbing, the talk-key handlers). Step 2 lists the parts whose Kotlin shape is not mechanical — the
class header, the field block, the observer object, the lifecycle methods and the message-log
accessors — and states the rule for everything else; Step 5 then lists, in full, every member the
behavior change touches. Nothing is elided inside a code block: what a block shows is the whole
member.

```kotlin
// app/src/test/java/se/lublin/mumla/service/MumlaServiceCharacterizationTest.kt
package se.lublin.mumla.service

import com.google.common.truth.Truth.assertThat
import org.junit.After
import org.junit.Before
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.runner.RunWith
import org.robolectric.Robolectric
import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
import org.robolectric.Shadows.shadowOf
import org.robolectric.android.controller.ServiceController
import se.lublin.humla.HumlaService

@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
class MumlaServiceCharacterizationTest {
    private lateinit var controller: ServiceController<MumlaService>
    private lateinit var service: MumlaService

    @Before
    fun setUp() {
        controller = Robolectric.buildService(MumlaService::class.java).create()
        service = controller.get()
    }

    @After
    fun tearDown() {
        controller.destroy()
    }

    @Test
    fun startsDisconnectedWithAnEmptyChatLogAndNoForegroundNotification() {
        assertThat(service.getConnectionState()).isEqualTo(HumlaService.ConnectionState.DISCONNECTED)
        assertThat(service.getMessageLog()).isEmpty()
        assertThat(shadowOf(service).lastForegroundNotification).isNull()
    }

    @Test
    fun loggedMessagesLandInTheChatLog() {
        service.logInfo("hello")
        service.logWarning("careful")
        service.logError("broken")
        shadowOf(android.os.Looper.getMainLooper()).idle()

        assertThat(service.getMessageLog().map { it.body }).containsExactly("careful", "broken")
    }

    @Test
    fun theMessageLogIsNotWritableThroughTheReturnedList() {
        service.logWarning("one")
        shadowOf(android.os.Looper.getMainLooper()).idle()

        val log = service.getMessageLog()
        assertThat(log).hasSize(1)
        service.clearMessageLog()
        assertThat(service.getMessageLog()).isEmpty()
    }
}
```

`logInfo` is dropped by `HumlaService.logInfo` until the session is synchronized, which is why only
the warning and the error are expected.

```kotlin
// app/src/test/java/se/lublin/mumla/service/MumlaServiceForegroundTest.kt
package se.lublin.mumla.service

import android.os.Bundle
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.Looper
import com.google.common.truth.Truth.assertThat
import com.google.protobuf.Message
import org.junit.After
import org.junit.Before
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.runner.RunWith
import org.robolectric.Robolectric
import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
import org.robolectric.Shadows.shadowOf
import org.robolectric.android.controller.ServiceController
import se.lublin.humla.HumlaService
import se.lublin.humla.model.Server
import se.lublin.humla.net.CryptState
import se.lublin.humla.net.HumlaConnection
import se.lublin.humla.net.HumlaSSLSocketFactory
import se.lublin.humla.net.HumlaTCP
import se.lublin.humla.net.HumlaTCPMessageType
import se.lublin.humla.net.HumlaUDP
import se.lublin.humla.net.TcpTransport
import se.lublin.humla.net.UdpTransport
import se.lublin.humla.session.SessionState
import se.lublin.humla.util.HumlaException
import se.lublin.mumla.R

@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
class MumlaServiceForegroundTest {
    /** Transports that open no socket and never call back; the session state is driven directly. */
    private class SilentTransports : HumlaConnection.TransportFactory {
        override fun createTcp(socketFactory: HumlaSSLSocketFactory, callbackHandler: Handler): TcpTransport =
            object : TcpTransport {
                override val isRunning: Boolean get() = false
                override fun setTCPConnectionListener(listener: HumlaTCP.TCPConnectionListener?) = Unit
                override fun connect(host: String, port: Int, useTor: Boolean) = Unit
                override fun sendMessage(message: Message, messageType: HumlaTCPMessageType) = Unit
                override fun sendMessage(data: ByteArray, length: Int, messageType: HumlaTCPMessageType) = Unit
                override fun disconnect() = Unit
            }

        override fun createUdp(
            cryptState: CryptState,
            listener: HumlaUDP.UDPConnectionListener,
            callbackHandler: Handler,
        ): UdpTransport = object : UdpTransport {
            override val isRunning: Boolean get() = false
            override fun connect(host: String, port: Int) = Unit
            override fun sendMessage(data: ByteArray, length: Int) = Unit
            override fun disconnect() = Unit
        }
    }

    private lateinit var controller: ServiceController<MumlaService>
    private lateinit var service: MumlaService
    private val mainLooper = shadowOf(Looper.getMainLooper())

    @Before
    fun setUp() {
        controller = Robolectric.buildService(MumlaService::class.java)
        service = controller.get()
        service.connectionFactory = { listener ->
            HumlaConnection(listener, SilentTransports(), Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()))
        }
        controller.create()
        mainLooper.idle()
    }

    @After
    fun tearDown() {
        controller.destroy()
    }

    private fun error() = HumlaException("socket reset", HumlaException.HumlaDisconnectReason.CONNECTION_ERROR)

    @Test
    fun theForegroundNotificationFollowsTheSessionState() {
        // Proves the collector is wired: connect() moves the state machine to Connecting before
        // any networking happens, and the notification must appear without anything else running.
        service.configureExtras(
            Bundle().apply { putParcelable(HumlaService.EXTRAS_SERVER, Server(-1, "test", "127.0.0.1", 64738, "me", "")) },
        )

        service.connect()
        mainLooper.idle()

        assertThat(shadowOf(service).lastForegroundNotification).isNotNull()
        assertThat(shadowOf(service).isForegroundStopped).isFalse()
    }

    @Test
    fun theForegroundStateSurvivesAConnectionLoss() {
        // Spec §6 regression test: "foreground kept across reconnect".
        service.renderSessionState(SessionState.Connecting)
        service.renderSessionState(SessionState.Connected)
        mainLooper.idle()
        assertThat(shadowOf(service).isForegroundStopped).isFalse()

        service.renderSessionState(SessionState.ConnectionLost(reconnectInMillis = 2_000, attempt = 1, error = error()))
        mainLooper.idle()

        assertThat(shadowOf(service).isForegroundStopped).isFalse()
        assertThat(shadowOf(service).lastForegroundNotification).isNotNull()

        service.renderSessionState(SessionState.Reconnecting)
        mainLooper.idle()
        assertThat(shadowOf(service).isForegroundStopped).isFalse()
    }

    @Test
    fun theChatLogSurvivesAConnectionLossAndIsClearedOnDisconnect() {
        service.renderSessionState(SessionState.Connecting)
        service.renderSessionState(SessionState.Connected)
        service.logWarning("something happened")
        mainLooper.idle()
        assertThat(service.getMessageLog()).hasSize(1)

        service.renderSessionState(SessionState.ConnectionLost(reconnectInMillis = 2_000, attempt = 1, error = error()))
        mainLooper.idle()
        assertThat(service.getMessageLog()).hasSize(1) // spec A3: not a Disconnected transition

        service.renderSessionState(SessionState.Disconnected(error()))
        mainLooper.idle()
        assertThat(service.getMessageLog()).isEmpty()
        assertThat(shadowOf(service).isForegroundStopped).isTrue()
    }

    @Test
    fun theChatLogIsBoundedAtFiveHundredEntries() {
        repeat(ChatMessageLog.MAX_ENTRIES + 1) { service.logWarning("m$it") }
        mainLooper.idle()

        assertThat(service.getMessageLog()).hasSize(ChatMessageLog.MAX_ENTRIES)
        assertThat(service.getMessageLog().first().body).isEqualTo("m1")
    }

    @Test
    fun aRefusedForegroundStartBecomesAWarningInsteadOfACrash() {
        service.renderSessionState(SessionState.Connecting)
        mainLooper.idle()
        service.notificationForTest()!!.foregroundStarter = { _, _ -> throw SecurityException("denied") }
        service.clearMessageLog()

        service.renderSessionState(SessionState.Connected) // re-enters the foreground
        mainLooper.idle()

        assertThat(service.getMessageLog().map { it.body })
            .contains(service.getString(R.string.foreground_start_failed)) // spec A6
    }

    @Test
    fun aHalfDuplexPreferenceChangeAlsoCarriesTheTransmitMode() {
        // Spec A7: the runtime change must not depend on EXTRAS_TRANSMIT_MODE being resent by
        // some other preference.
        val preferences = androidx.preference.PreferenceManager.getDefaultSharedPreferences(service)
        preferences.edit()
            .putString(Settings.PREF_INPUT_METHOD, Settings.ARRAY_INPUT_METHOD_PTT)
            .putBoolean(Settings.PREF_HALF_DUPLEX, true)
            .commit()

        service.onSharedPreferenceChanged(preferences, Settings.PREF_HALF_DUPLEX)
        mainLooper.idle()

        assertThat(service.getAudioConfigForTest().halfDuplex).isTrue()
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Drive the foreground from the session state and bound the chat log**

Apply these edits to `MumlaService.kt`.

1. Add the state collector. Fields and `onCreate`/`onDestroy`:

```kotlin
    private val mServiceScope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + Dispatchers.Main.immediate)
```

```kotlin
    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        registerObserver(mObserver)

        // Register for preference changes
        mSettings = Settings.getInstance(this)
        mPTTSoundEnabled = mSettings.isPttSoundEnabled
        mShortTtsMessagesEnabled = mSettings.isShortTextToSpeechMessagesEnabled
        val preferences = PreferenceManager.getDefaultSharedPreferences(this)
        preferences.registerOnSharedPreferenceChangeListener(this)

        // Manually set theme to style overlay views
        // XML <application> theme does NOT do this!
        setTheme(R.style.Theme_Mumla)

        mMessageLog = ChatMessageLog()
        mMessageNotification = MumlaMessageNotification(this)

        // Instantiate overlay view
        mChannelOverlay = MumlaOverlay(this)
        mHotCorner = MumlaHotCorner(this, mSettings.hotCornerGravity, mHotCornerListener)

        // Set up TTS
        if (mSettings.isTextToSpeechEnabled) mTTS = TextToSpeech(this, mTTSInitListener)

        mTalkReceiver = TalkBroadcastReceiver(this)

        // Spec A3/A6: the notification is a function of the session state, and only a
        // Disconnected state tears the foreground down.
        mServiceScope.launch { getSessionState().collect { renderSessionState(it) } }
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        mServiceScope.cancel()
        mNotification?.hide()
        mNotification = null
        mReconnectNotification?.hide()
        mReconnectNotification = null

        val preferences = PreferenceManager.getDefaultSharedPreferences(this)
        preferences.unregisterOnSharedPreferenceChangeListener(this)
        try {
            unregisterReceiver(mTalkReceiver)
        } catch (e: IllegalArgumentException) {
            Log.d(TAG, "Talk receiver was not registered")
        }

        unregisterObserver(mObserver)
        mTTS?.shutdown()
        mMessageLog = null
        mMessageNotification.dismiss()
        super.onDestroy()
    }
```

   Imports: `kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope`, `kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers`,
   `kotlinx.coroutines.SupervisorJob`, `kotlinx.coroutines.cancel`, `kotlinx.coroutines.launch`,
   `se.lublin.humla.session.SessionState`.

2. Add the renderer and its helpers; this is the only place that shows or hides the foreground
   notification:

```kotlin
    /**
     * Renders one session state into the foreground notification (spec A3, A6).
     *
     * `Connecting` starts the foreground; `Connected` only changes the text and adds the actions;
     * `ConnectionLost`/`Reconnecting` keep it and say so; only `Disconnected` tears it down, along
     * with the chat log and the chat notification.
     */
    internal fun renderSessionState(state: SessionState) {
        when (state) {
            SessionState.Connecting -> {
                mReconnectNotification?.hide()
                mReconnectNotification = null
                mErrorShown = false
                showConnectionNotification(getString(R.string.mumlaConnecting) + torSuffix(), actions = false)
            }
            SessionState.Connected ->
                showConnectionNotification(getString(R.string.connected) + torSuffix(), actions = true)
            is SessionState.ConnectionLost, SessionState.Reconnecting ->
                showConnectionNotification(getString(R.string.connection_lost_reconnecting), actions = false)
            is SessionState.Disconnected -> {
                mNotification?.hide()
                mNotification = null
                clearMessageLog()
                mMessageNotification.dismiss()
                val error = state.error
                if (error != null && !mSuppressNotifications) {
                    mReconnectNotification = MumlaReconnectNotification.show(
                        this, error.message + torSuffix(), false, this,
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun torSuffix(): String = if (mSettings.isTorEnabled) " (Tor)" else ""

    private fun showConnectionNotification(contentText: String, actions: Boolean) {
        val notification = mNotification
            ?: MumlaConnectionNotification.create(this, contentText, this).also { mNotification = it }
        notification.customContentText = contentText
        notification.actionsShown = actions
        if (!notification.show()) {
            // Spec A6: the platform refused the foreground start. Say so in the chat log and fall
            // back to the reconnect notification instead of letting the service die.
            logWarning(getString(R.string.foreground_start_failed))
            mNotification = null
            if (!mSuppressNotifications) {
                mReconnectNotification = MumlaReconnectNotification.show(
                    this, getString(R.string.foreground_start_failed), true, this,
                )
            }
        }
    }

    /** Test seam for the A6 failure path. */
    internal fun notificationForTest(): MumlaConnectionNotification? = mNotification
```

3. Delete the notification handling from `mObserver`: `onConnecting()` and `onConnected()` lose
   their bodies entirely (the renderer does that work now) and are removed from the object;
   `onDisconnected(e)` is removed as well. `onUserStateUpdated` keeps its mute/deafen text update
   but goes through the renderer's helper:

```kotlin
        override fun onUserStateUpdated(user: IUser?) {
            if (user == null) return

            val selfSession = try {
                getSessionId()
            } catch (e: IllegalStateException) {
                Log.d(TAG, "exception in onUserStateUpdated: $e")
                return
            }

            if (user.getSession() == selfSession) {
                mSettings.setMutedAndDeafened(user.isSelfMuted, user.isSelfDeafened)
                val contentText = when {
                    user.isSelfMuted && user.isSelfDeafened -> getString(R.string.status_notify_muted_and_deafened)
                    user.isSelfMuted -> getString(R.string.status_notify_muted)
                    else -> getString(R.string.connected)
                }
                if (mNotification != null) showConnectionNotification(contentText, actions = true)
            }

            if (user.getTextureHash() != null && user.getTexture() == null) {
                // Update avatar data if available.
                requestAvatar(user.getSession())
            }
        }
```

4. Replace `onConnectionDisconnected` so it no longer tears the session's user-visible state down
   on a loss that is going to be retried:

```kotlin
    override fun onConnectionDisconnected(e: HumlaException?) {
        super.onConnectionDisconnected(e)
        try {
            unregisterReceiver(mTalkReceiver)
        } catch (iae: IllegalArgumentException) {
            // Not registered.
        }

        // Remove overlay if present.
        mChannelOverlay.hide()
        mHotCorner.setShown(false)
        setProximitySensorOn(false)

        // The chat log and the chat notification are session-visible state: spec A3 keeps them
        // across a ConnectionLost, and renderSessionState clears them when the state really
        // becomes Disconnected.
    }
```

5. Replace the message-log members:

```kotlin
    override fun getMessageLog(): List<IChatMessage> = mMessageLog?.snapshot() ?: emptyList()

    override fun clearMessageLog() {
        mMessageLog?.clear()
    }

    /** Test seam: the bounded log behind [getMessageLog]. */
    internal fun messageLogForTest(): ChatMessageLog? = mMessageLog
```

   and every `mMessageLog.add(...)` in the observer and in the two `sendUserTextMessage`/
   `sendChannelTextMessage` overrides becomes `mMessageLog?.add(...)`.

6. `onSharedPreferenceChanged`: carry the transmit mode with the half-duplex change (spec A7):

```kotlin
            Settings.PREF_HALF_DUPLEX -> {
                changedExtras.putBoolean(HumlaService.EXTRAS_HALF_DUPLEX, mSettings.isHalfDuplex)
                // Half duplex only applies to push-to-talk, so the service needs the current
                // transmit mode in the same bundle to decide (spec A7).
                changedExtras.putInt(HumlaService.EXTRAS_TRANSMIT_MODE, mSettings.humlaInputMethod)
            }
```

7. `cancelReconnect()` keeps hiding the reconnect notification and calls `super.cancelReconnect()`;
   no change beyond the Kotlin syntax.

## Self-review (writing-plans)

Run this before handing the plan over; it is the same checklist the skill prescribes.

- **Spec coverage.** Every requirement of spec §3.2 has a task: A1 → Tasks 2, 4, 5
  (`fiveThousandChannelStatesDoNotStallAMainLooperTaskBeyond16ms` is the §6 regression test);
  A2 → Task 7 + Task 9 Step 5 items 3, 6 (`disconnectDoesNotBlockTheMainThreadWhileAudioTearsDown`);
  A3 → Task 1 + Task 9 Step 5 items 5, 6 + Task 12 (`renderSessionState`);
  A4 → Task 8 + Task 9 Step 5 item 8 (`bluetoothScoIsRestartedAfterAReconnect`);
  A5 → Tasks 3 and 6; A6 → Tasks 11 and 12 (`theForegroundStateSurvivesAConnectionLoss`,
  `aRefusedForegroundStartBecomesAWarningInsteadOfACrash`);
  A7 → Task 9 (`halfDuplexOnlyAppliesToPushToTalk`) + Task 12
  (`aHalfDuplexPreferenceChangeAlsoCarriesTheTransmitMode`);
  A8 → Task 7 + Task 9 (`anAudioWarningReachesTheChatLog`, `aConnectionWarningReachesTheChatLog`).
  Spec §4: the nine `EXTRAS_*` keys, `IHumlaService.getSessionState()` and
  `IHumlaSession.isBluetoothScoActive()` are Task 9 Step 5 items 10–12; `AudioHandler.Builder`
  additions and `captureState` are stream B's, with the A-side forwarding in place (Cross-stream
  touches item 3). Spec §6: three of the four regression tests are in this plan (main-thread stall,
  Bluetooth after reconnect, foreground across reconnect) plus the service-level "no main-thread
  join"; the fourth (VAD on the preprocessed signal) belongs to stream B. Every file in the file
  map is created, converted or modified by a numbered step.
- **Placeholders.** No `TBD`, no `...` inside a code block, no "similar to task N": search the file
  for `TBD`, `FIXME:` outside the two carried-over upstream comments, and `\.\.\.` before handing
  over.
- **Type consistency.** `SessionState`, `SessionStateMachine`, `ReconnectPolicy`, `AudioConfig`
  (`legacyEchoCancellationMethod` vs `echoCancellationMode`), `AudioSessionParams`, `ManagedAudio`,
  `AudioHandlerFactory`, `AudioController`, `CommunicationDevices`, `ScoRouter`, `TcpTransport`,
  `UdpTransport`, `MessageHandlerRegistry`, `ConnectionWarning`, `UdpHealthMonitor`,
  `ChatMessageLog` and `MumlaConnectionNotification` are spelled identically in every task that
  names them, and the fakes (`FakeTcpTransport`, `FakeUdpTransport`, `FakeTransports`,
  `RecordingConnectionListener`, `FakeAudio`, `FakeAudioFactory`, `FakeCommunicationDevices`,
  `HumlaServiceHarness`) are declared once and reused.
- **Commit messages.** Conventional Commits, English, imperative, subject ≤ 72 characters, detail
  in `-m` bodies, and **no trailers** — no `Co-Authored-By`, no `Claude-Session`, no
  `Signed-off-by`.
